# Research audit status

The thirteen historical root figures and their analytical conclusions remain
**legacy, not revalidated findings**. The current regenerated research graphics
inspect source/model evidence; they do not certify a classifier, complete
coverage or party-policy findings.

## State-local exposure sensitivity: October 29

The national maximum of 0.0111 percentage points is **not a bound for individual
states**. A separate, complete state-level replay finds Pennsylvania's largest
unanimity change is -1.7857143 points in International affairs; majority changes
by +4.5454545 points in Government operations. Its diagnostic subset has 44
contexts versus 56 full contexts, removing 12 known matches, including three
body gaps. These are small, selected cohorts, not statewide legislative volumes.

All 2,100 state/topic/rule rows, 1,200 state/status rows and 100 peak rows pass
independent pandas/Parquet reconstruction. All 617 complete removed records
match the original saved decisions. The two actual 4050x3900 PNG backgrounds
are inspected; all 100 displayed changes, 50 denominator labels and editable
SVG geometry/text are verified. Forty-seven states have nonzero changes under
each rule; CT and MA have observed zero changes, while Maine has no cohort
and remains unavailable. All candidate contexts within each state, including
missing bodies and abstentions, enter the denominators.

Accepted report: `data/state-exposure-sensitivity-20261029-v1/`.
Proofs: `data/state-exposure-sensitivity-qa-20261029-v1/`.
The original national producer/reports and canonical 27-chart collection remain
unchanged. No source acquisition, inference, training, human annotations,
cohort migration, scientific promotion or production integration occurs.
This tests only descriptive sensitivity to known saved-label matches, not
accuracy, independence or the causal effect of training. Commands and exact
source pin: [state-local replay](REPRODUCIBILITY.md#state-local-exposure-sensitivity).
All 36 affected tests, full repository lint, 231 public-doc checks and 57
relative links/anchors pass. All 85 frozen controls, 145 canonical graph
artifacts, original national reports and producer are unchanged. The initial
synthetic source-directory guard failure and exact sources/logs are retained;
only successful fixtures are cleaned. The known 34 legacy artifact failures
remain; the full legacy audit is not repeated for this diagnostic.

## Full-population saved-label exposure census: October 29

The current retained-text population must not be interpreted as a held-out test.
A new source-bound auditor streams all 157,598 contexts and 179,849 views against
the current model's exact 808-training / 50-development saved-label file.

| Exposure channel | Contexts | Retained views |
|---|---:|---:|
| Exact same-domain training text | 65 | 65 |
| Normalized training text, including exact | 65 | 82 |
| Training bill identity | 610 | 820 |
| Development bill identity | 7 | 11 |

Sixty-eight training-seen bill identities have no retained body; the identity
channel does not silently drop them. Development text has zero exact or
normalized matches. Channels overlap and must not be added. Same bill does not
establish the same version/input; normalization is advisory, not exact encoder
input or semantic near-duplicate equivalence.

The actual report accounts all 50 states, the original 139,869 contexts with
bodies / 17,729 without, and three explicitly excluded views. Complete matched
join ledgers retain 831 views / 617 contexts with source-file/physical-line,
hash and saved-reference mappings. These ledgers are not substitute full-query
downloads; complete original evidence and existing inspector exports remain
unchanged. Three small exports total 583,262 bytes, without copying the population.

A separate computational pass reproduces all population exact-text and bill-ID
joins, every emitted normalized match, missingness and state counts. It does not
independently repeat normalization over unmatched views or supply human gold.
Focused tests cover that normalization algorithm. **No match is not proof of
independence:** the census excludes other consumed diagnostics, semantic near
duplicates, related bill families and pretraining exposure.

All 72 targeted tests, full repository lint, 227 public-doc checks and all 85
frozen controls pass. The canonical audit is 469/503: only the two new Python
code-guide checks change, with the same 34 failures and all non-counter fields
unchanged. Source/model/label/human/cohort/graph bytes and scientific/publication
status are unchanged; no inference, collection or population snapshot occurred.
Commands: [reproducibility guide](REPRODUCIBILITY.md#population-training-exposure-census).
Proofs: `data/population-training-exposure-20261029-v1/`.

## Repairs from the project overview: October 28

Four engineering problems are repaired without supplying invented research data:

- The canonical audit no longer overwrites the historical report by default.
  Default stdout is JSON; explicit reports require fresh exclusive creation.
  Existing files, symlinks and concurrent creators are preserved, with real
  missing-artifact failures still returning a nonzero exit.
- Its ignored-file check now queries only the seven named authored inputs,
  rather than enumerating the entire ignored research tree. The first actual
  unscoped attempt timed out at 240 seconds; the scoped default run completed
  in 1.005 seconds. Both ignored and nonignored fixtures retain their correct
  dispositions; the timed-out attempt is preserved.
- Broad legacy `all`, `analysis` and `legacy-figures` builds now require
  `ALLOW_LEGACY_ANALYSIS=1` before any dependencies can run, including parallel
  Make. Current saved-table rendering and `audit-current` remain separate.
- All 12 NJ-producer lint violations are repaired. AST comparison preserves
  all 58 other functions and every previous global constant; only the two
  loaders' exact producer-compatibility guards change. Exact prior SHA/size
  bindings preserve existing archives without waiving source, payload, reader,
  metadata or approval replay. Archived producer code is not executed.

The actual default and explicitly saved audits both remain **465/499 passing,
34 failing**, covering the same 28 missing legacy paths. Every non-counter field
equals the prior full-project audit. An existing-report collision exits before
data checks, and the historical report remains byte-identical.
All **194 affected regressions** pass. All six original NJ packets also complete
full source/dual-reader/text/span/metadata/approval replay with unchanged
payload fingerprints: original text 14 contexts / 40 views; full collection
97/255; tail 6/16; effective-date 10/26; single-page 3/8; printed-variant 19/53.
These are per-packet replay counts, not a sum of new independent records or
newly recovered evidence. No archived producer code is executed.

All 85 frozen human/model/runtime controls and the inspector remain unchanged.
The original current graph collection was verified and independently preserved
before the separately documented PLOT-03 presentation update above.
Full repository lint now passes. Code checkpoint `3a598ca` is saved/pushed as
Hans Hanley; the original six-packet replay job completed successfully.

## Full-project audit: October 28

**The project is not complete as a scientific study.** The current research
bundles and engineering safeguards are substantially implemented, but source
eligibility, operative platforms, event-time affiliations and independent human
validation still prevent certified party-policy findings and publication.
See [Project status and gaps](PROJECT_STATUS.md) for a plain-language overview.

| Surface | Fresh result |
|---|---|
| Complete offline code run | **2,469 passed**, zero failures/errors/skips; all three real Chromium cycle-browser fixtures ran. One duplicate artifact assertion was deselected only to avoid overwriting the historical report; the actual artifact audit ran separately below. |
| Canonical artifact gate | **463/497 checks pass; 34 fail**, covering **28 distinct missing legacy paths**. The failure list and every non-counter report field exactly match the earlier storage-checkpoint audit. This gate still targets legacy 2018+/MiniLM outputs, not a unified current-study rebuild. |
| Full Python lint | **12 existing violations** in `bills/nj_library_collection.py` and `bills/nj_library_text.py`: eleven line-length diagnostics and one simplification diagnostic. These pinned source producers were not reformatted during a documentation/audit task. |
| Browser JavaScript | All four shipped JavaScript files pass syntax checks. |
| Six current research bundles | All **355 artifact paths / 30,018,470,166 logical bytes** freshly rehashed with unchanged file fingerprints and manifest pins; no copying, acquisition or population inference. |
| Current charts | All **108 exports / 35 complete CSVs and ZIP members / 3,176 marks** reconcile independently. Both background geometries match; 13 legacy PNGs remain unchanged. Current majority and source-coverage PNGs were also visually inspected. |
| Cohort and missing records | All **157,598 original contexts**, all **17,729 missing-body IDs** and **939,637 original metadata-field comparisons** reconcile. All original context admission flags remain false. |
| Frozen controls after the suite | All **85** human-plan/runtime/model/label controls and the original canonical report remain unchanged. Synthetic test models and annotations are isolated fixtures, not a new research-model run or real human labels. |

The fresh record audit distinguishes **50 accounted states from 49 states with
eligible contexts**. Maine has zero primary-cohort contexts; Massachusetts has
one, Pennsylvania 56 and New York 860. These are selection/eligibility counts,
not official state legislative volumes. Unresolved event evidence and
calendar-versus-session boundaries must not be hidden by reporting only the
17,729 missing bodies within the selected cohort. Do not invent dates or
silently migrate the primary population to repair this limitation.

The 28-input human packet remains explicitly unannotated, not human or
independent gold. Current majority assignments remain unpromoted; neither
integrity checks nor additional assignments establish accuracy. All 100
organizations' operative-platform coverage remains unresolved, and historical
party/source-version/rights/report prerequisites remain open. No production
website changes or approvals were made.

Fresh local proofs are under `data/full-project-audit-20261028-v1/`:
`tests.xml`, `artifacts.json`, `lint.log`, `javascript.json`, `bundles.json`,
`current-graphs.json`, `cohort-v2.json` and `preservation.json`.
The original failed cohort observer and its log remain preserved: it wrongly
assumed every accounted state had an eligible context; the corrected observer
changes that assumption, not the data. Large research artifacts remain local
and ignored; a code push is not their backup.

## Longer-context and title/body experiments: October 24, 2026

Both bounded experiments are complete, **not promoted**. The frozen checkpoint
supports 8,192 positions but was trained with 1,024-token windows and 128-token
overlap. Increasing inference context does not retrain or recalibrate it.
The pilot retains all **109 selected original contexts / 255 entire source
views / 9,953,777 policy characters**, with no discarded tokens or source
alternatives. It freezes two profiles before forward passes: 4,096/512 and
8,192/1,024 tokens/overlap, retaining the strict-majority rule and its declared
supporting-window quality gates.

| Selected-cohort policy | Assigned | Same assistant proposal | Different proposal | Advisory unresolved |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 1,024-token majority | 70 | 58 | 10 | 2 |
| 4,096-token majority | 74 | 59 | 13 | 2 |
| 8,192-token majority | 79 | 61 | 16 | 2 |

The 4,096 profile adds six assignments but loses two existing assignments;
the 8,192 profile adds ten but loses one. Neither changes a previously accepted
topic into a different accepted topic. Keeping every primary assignment and
using longer windows only for unresolved cases instead yields 76 or 80
assignments, but fourteen or seventeen disagreements with the assistant
proposals. Of the six new 4,096 assignments, two match proposals and four differ;
of the ten new 8,192 assignments, three match and seven differ. These are
development diagnostics on a selected, already inspected cohort, not
representative or independent human accuracy. Neither the model nor the
assistant proposals are adjudicated truth.

The explicit runtime never edits the checkpoint manifest or substitutes a
cached short-document decision for a larger window's logits. Every cache key
binds model run, complete input SHA, token interval and actual encoder IDs.

original and 1,208 newly encoded windows, with 768 atomic batch receipts.
The 4,096 profile encodes 830 new windows in 139.835 encoder seconds; the 8,192
profile encodes 378 in 207.395 seconds. These measured forward times exclude
tokenization, archive building and verification. A completed resume verifies
all payload/archive bindings without loading the encoder or changing the cache.

## Strict-majority bill-topic candidate: October 24, 2026

The requested majority alternative is implemented and executed for the entire
saved population. **Strictly more than half** of all window argmax votes must
support one output; a plurality or tie cannot assign it. The 0.80 score / 0.20
margin gates apply to the complete mean vector of supporting windows. This is
an intentional change of gate semantics, not a claim that the whole-document
mean remains above 0.80. Source aggregation then requires strictly more than
half of distinct exact input strings to yield the same accepted output.
Uncertain sources remain in the denominator; learned rejection is a separate
output, not an inferred fact that the bill is out of scope.

Actual full-population results:

| Recorded decision | Frozen unanimity | Strict-majority candidate |
|---|---:|---:|
| Topic assigned | 65,091 | 78,300 |
| Learned model rejection | 23,198 | 23,618 |
| Other unresolved decisions | 51,025 | 37,396 |
| Source-test exclusions | 3 | 3 |
| No supplied text | 18,281 | 18,281 |

The **13,209 new provisional assignments** originate from 10,711 window-veto
cases, 2,479 source-alternative cases and nineteen mixed-reason cases.
Available-but-unassigned contexts fall from **74,226 to 61,017**, a 17.8%
reduction, **not an accuracy improvement claim**. Every one of the 65,091
previously accepted topics is preserved unchanged. All 157,598 entire original
context rows and 178,745 entire original predictions survive outside an added
candidate namespace. The run uses all 785,550 saved windows, no encoder calls
and no changed source/model weights.

All 348 exact duplicate source votes are deweighted. The candidate explicitly
retains minority evidence: 1,042 assigned contexts have source disagreement or
uncertainty, and 12,880 have within-document disagreement. Eleven majority
assignments differ from the argmax of the unconditional mean vector. These are
reported consequences of hard voting, not silently represented as soft pooling.
Different extraction strings are not certified independent editions, and the
overlapping model windows are not independent human semantic judgments.

The selected assistant-only casebook is diagnostic, not human gold. Of 32
newly assigned contexts in that selected cohort, 25 match its proposals, six
differ and one has no advisory consensus. Across all 109 cases the candidate
has 58 same-topic comparisons, ten differing topics, 37 unassigned-with-proposal,
two unresolved in both and two assignments without advisory consensus.
The six new differences require real adjudication; they are neither proved
errors nor grounds for silently editing the advisory labels or model.
The frozen checkpoint records **808 training / 50 dev examples, all marked
assistant-reviewed**, and was trained with complete-document mean-probability
loss. Majority postprocessing is a different decision policy and cannot inherit
independent calibration or validation from that training run.

The actual gallery is `outputs/topic-majority-2023-2024-v1/`: 23 artifacts,
three PNG/SVG pairs and a complete **123-member research ZIP** preserving all
108 original inference members. ZIP: **2,131,586,639 bytes**, SHA-256
`a837710c39ece99608b22ad2c7bd47eb53e9d014d3fdd4ff0ac7cc8b528cdeec`.
Manifest:
`ea392fae9eed070199e47d894f6a93c15ac4bc4af1e4aeb470676b565afe6845`.
The policy is frozen before execution under
`bbf6f887cbed4f703416e90723c9e015292dc582784007756c27f774c5cb223a`.
Twenty focused majority/diagnostic regressions pass. An independent observer
imports neither new implementation nor producer and reconstructs every window,
source/context vote and all six chart tables, preserving every original field.
Actual desktop/mobile Chromium checks decode all three SVGs and verify nine
byte-identical downloads, including the complete ZIP, with zero JavaScript
errors or external requests. All browser helpers exit.

The actual full audit is **441/475**: three newly documented Python files add

failures and every other report field remain identical. The missing old

This candidate does not rewrite v19, the old galleries, frozen human plan,

## October 21 engineering assurance recheck

The fresh repository-wide code run passed **1,641 tests**, including configured real

original report; the actual audit ran separately to a new report and passed **340/374
checks**, retaining the **exact same 34 missing-artifact failures**. This is not a
green end-to-end research audit.

The broader run exposed a source-audit bottleneck: filtering `rglob` results still
traversed the enormous excluded data tree. The local-only compute guard now prunes
excluded directories before descent. A regression fails on the prior implementation
and verifies that nested source violations remain detectable without entering excluded
trees. All eleven local-only guard tests pass; the actual repository source scan took
0.12 seconds in this recheck. Imports, endpoints and model policies are unchanged.

Fresh verification also bound **1,488 served artifacts**, **1,042 original input
digests**, and the frozen human-evaluation artifacts, runtime sources and original
model weights. All five actual local workspace routes and the complete 84-record Texas
query download passed in Chromium; helpers were stopped. The 28-input human packet
remains unannotated. Python lint, both browser-script syntax checks and all 149 public
documentation checks passed. Detailed receipts are saved locally under
`data/assurance-*20261021-v*.{json,xml,log}`; source bundles and scientific gates are
unchanged. No model promotion, publication or additional state completion is implied.

**October 21 scope reduction:** the active study is now **January 1, 2023 through
December 31, 2024**, choosing the earlier of the user's two permitted cycles.
Only legislative measures introduced/filed in that window and platforms applicable
during it are target evidence; keep bill/non-bill types distinct. Respect actual publisher session boundaries and retain
special-session distinctions; overlapping biennia need record-level inclusion,
not substring filtering. Unknown filing dates remain unknown. Pre-2023 platforms
need evidence of continued applicability; 2025–2026 platforms cannot replace them.

Wider archives, historical OCR, older date/identity exceptions and multi-cycle
trends are no longer completion requirements. Preserve the accepted packets and
external classifier-training material; do not extend their temporal coverage.
Reuse target-cycle subsets without recollecting accepted sources. This changes
the research contract, not canonical bytes, frozen reports or certification:
existing wide-window exports have not been filtered or regenerated.
Florida's accepted multi-year linked cohort does not establish the newly selected
cycle's native universe; its tracker status is therefore Partial. The six other
accepted source scopes can supply reusable 2023–2024 evidence, with their existing
exceptions. Historical counts and seven-scope statements below retain their
original checkpoint meaning, not current dispatch priority.

## Explicit conflict-exclusion experiment

The fail-closed training-preparation default is unchanged: conflicting eligible
external labels still raise an error. A new, explicitly selected
`--conflict-policy exclude-groups` experiment instead excludes **every eligible
member of each conflicting normalized-text group before deduplication and
sampling**. It never selects the first/majority topic, rewrites a source label
or treats ambiguity as a reviewed rejection. Complete original excluded rows,
source positions, competing codes and hashes are retained for audit.

The actual new packet
`data/labels/cap-conflict-exclusion-experiment-20261021-v1/` excludes **2,228
groups / 6,120 eligible source rows**, including the groups exposing 116
canonical training records. Resampling supplies **6,103 training rows**, with
the same 263 retained assistant-reviewed records and 50-row development set.
The original 6,158-row canonical manifest is byte- and stat-unchanged. New labels
SHA-256: `2b9d0c833846341c7ef94b72b2358c9033ef4aceba0e8ebb6d7be2b3407393ed`.
Preparation manifest:
`66a75a42301b0a1d54e1f5f5b5586eed981f90ef08e1f36024cf75b7afc2a849`.

The 52 previously examined challenge records remain source/group/text-isolated;
no new training example is an exact or detected near copy. All wrapped training
inputs fit the retained 1,024-token budget (maximum 468). Whole-group exclusion
is an explicit conservative experimental choice, not adjudicated gold or
certification of remaining labels. Fresh full-encoder/head training completed,
with a verified encoder parameter change. At the unchanged confidence .8,
margin .2 and temperature 1 policy, its development decision accuracy was 54%
versus 62% for the prior Republican curriculum; the previously examined
52-input challenge was 88.46% versus 94.23%. Exclusion repairs label integrity,
not demonstrated accuracy. Neither model is promoted.

## Target-cycle classification diagnostics and curriculum

A frozen **190-input complete-metadata diagnostic** accounts for all 50 states:
110 topic decisions and 80 abstentions. Maine has no candidate; MA and MT had
one each in that frozen snapshot. These are manually read, model-blind
assistant references, **not independent human gold or a population sample**.
After inspecting five models' results, this is a development diagnostic,
not an untouched final holdout. At the same fixed decision policy, the best
decision accuracy was only **61.58%** (joint target rejection); the
conflict-exclusion model reached 57.37%. All remain provisional and the
default loader refuses them. Actual results and full decision records are in
`data/processed/cycle-metadata-blind-evaluation-20261021-v1/`.

The earlier corpus had only 51 positive reviewed bill examples. Two separate
GPT-6.1 Sol reviewers therefore read **564 new complete target inputs**, with
the 190 diagnostic and earlier challenge/source/group reservations excluded
before sampling. Decisions comprise 410 topics, 75 non-policy,
45 insufficient-evidence and 34 rubric-ambiguity cases. The 34 ambiguous
cases remain in explicit quarantine, not arbitrary winning labels; one
1,538-token unit is separately preserved rather than truncated for training.

The composed experimental dataset has **6,632 training / 50 development
records**, retaining the original initialization/review records plus 529 new
bounded target units. Canonical 6,158-row labels remain unchanged. An explicit
reviewed-target sampling weight of 8 addresses external-source dominance
without altering the default trainer's original epoch shuffle. A separate
three-epoch full encoder/head experiment uses a pre-frozen evaluation plan;
its results cannot certify independent accuracy or isolate a causal effect of
sampling because the curriculum also changed.

That run completed with a verified encoder update and finite losses
1.04416, .49240 and .30613. At the frozen policy it reached **61.05%** on the
190 cycle inputs, **92.31%** on the earlier 52-input platform challenge and
**60%** on development 50. Its cycle classified precision was **58.73%**:
74 correct topics among 126 classified inputs, plus 42 correct abstentions.
This does not surpass the prior best cycle decision result; **no model is
promoted**. Full decisions, source/weight immutability and default
provisional-loader refusal are retained in
`data/processed/cycle-target-balanced-evaluation-20261021-v1/`.

## Complete native-input review, not metadata-only validation

A separate source-reserved packet now freezes **39 complete native inputs /
143,398 characters**, with 20 states sampled and all 50 accounted for.
All selected bills' original reader/reference alternatives remain complete.
Two isolated GPT-6.1 Sol reviewers each read all 39 exact inputs before any
model predictions. They agreed on **36**; the **three disagreements are
preserved without a winning label and excluded from the primary score**.
This is independent assistant execution, not independent human gold.
The character-bounded diagnostic is not representative population sampling.

At the predeclared unchanged policy, the joint-target model reached **55.56%
decision accuracy / 68.18% classified precision** on the agreed inputs; the
balanced target model reached **72.22% / 86.36%**, respectively. The latter
classified 22/36, with 19 correct topics and seven correct abstentions.
All 39-input disagreement sensitivities remain separate. The inference
preserved **37,326 tokens / 57 windows**, including 12 multichunk inputs,
with zero discarded tokens. Neither model is promoted.

Actual evidence:
`data/labels/cycle-native-blind-review-20261021-v1/` and
`data/processed/cycle-native-input-diagnostic-20261021-v1/`.
Frozen pre-review plan:
`cffe58358b988566ad318205977c0be7f92a3dd25176bd0b60795d0a74ca321e`.
Evaluation manifest:
`2ea3479d0c5e8285b16996758f52e15aab96dc1701d40a044902da6f13ef11f3`.
These results have now informed development, so this is not an untouched
final holdout. Comparing them with the different metadata sample is not a
causal estimate of text-source improvement.

The prior trainer could not use complete over-budget supervision. New opt-in
`--allow-document-windows` trains **one complete-unit likelihood over all
bounded windows**, not separately propagated chunk labels. Default refusal
and strict inference consensus remain unchanged. A disjoint review of
68 further native inputs retains 50 topics, 15 non-policy units and three
unresolved rubric cases outside training. Composition preserves all 6,682
prior rows, adds 65 native units and restores the complete 1,538-token unit.
The new corpus has **6,698 train / 50 dev**, with exact preflight coverage of
275,643 source tokens / 6,728 windows / 23 multichunk units. Nothing is
truncated; encoding coverage does not assert every unit was sampled by the
weighted optimizer. The actual three-epoch run completed, updated its encoder
and preserved all producer/taxonomy bytes. Losses were 1.10301, .45965 and
.31895. Deterministic optimizer replay verifies all 65 new native units were
sampled and the restored long unit was used 17 times.

At the unchanged policy, this model reached **65.79%** on metadata 190,
**75%** on native agreed 36, **86.54%** on platform 52 and **54%** on dev 50.
Native classified precision was **84%** at 25/36 coverage (21 correct topics,
six correct abstentions). These are tradeoffs, not a universal improvement:
native precision and platform decisions regress relative to retained
alternatives. The model is not promoted. Evaluation manifest:
`5853e29cc746f51fee427bbfa7d090878286d74d39cd82fa4b1cb0c24b73555b`.

A final explicit reviewed-only ablation now tests whether remaining external
label/target-taxonomy mismatch is dominating training. It preserves all
858 reviewed rows (808 train / 50 dev) and separately retains all 5,890
external rows without relabeling/deletion. All 21 topics have reviewed
positives, although the rarest has only two. Its opt-in uniform class loss
and full epoch shuffle are recorded; default inverse-frequency loss is
unchanged. Complete preflight retains 94,935 tokens / 838 windows / 23
multichunk units. Six-epoch training completed under frozen plan
`c87a9792e787d8d59d7234a11c84a86dc766b032fcab27b0471cd5e78f2b4f9a`.
The encoder changed, all 808 training units were sampled exactly once per
epoch, all 65 new native units participated and the restored long unit was
used six times. Finite losses were 2.47723, 1.28487, .44147, .13042, .05888
and .02039; declining training loss is not evidence of generalization.

At the same frozen inference policy, the completed comparison is:

| Development diagnostic | Full-document mixed curriculum: decision accuracy | Reviewed-only: decision accuracy |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Metadata 190 | 65.79% | 62.11% |
| Native agreed 36 | 75.00% | 72.22% |
| Platform 52 | 86.54% | 75.00% |
| Development 50 | 54.00% | 56.00% |

Reviewed-only native classified precision rose to **94.74%**, but only
**19/36** inputs were classified (18 correct topics, plus eight correct
abstentions). The mixed model classified 25/36 at 84% precision. This is a
precision/coverage tradeoff on a small assistant-reviewed diagnostic, not
population accuracy or a universally better classifier. External removal,
exposure and loss weighting changed jointly, so their causal effects are
not isolated. No references or thresholds were changed, no model is
promoted and no further tuning on these consumed diagnostics is planned.
Training manifest:
`2660bca48e99a27058724d3a1e0113e3492c6ef48a974b2acbde729fe131d127`.
Evaluation manifest:
`6c2c589bc867620bf37b065c0390c343d3cfb5a198d19726310a7f7656e95319`.
Full predictions and retained producers are in
`data/processed/cycle-reviewed-only-evaluation-20261021-v1/`.
No untouched-holdout, human-gold, causal or release claim is made.

## Independent reference recovery: bounded negative, not invented gold

The retained four-path provider investigation recovered no verified
independent 2023–2024 state-topic evaluation reference within that scope.
This is not a universal nonexistence finding. The actual capture preserves
12 complete provider responses, methods/codebooks and full cycle records:
`data/processed/independent-state-label-discovery-2023-2024-v1/`,
manifest `d854bf0e6575a5c41187b81e14f6381d60bca0f50cef9e81ab1b2f8b9f05a439`.

Its 6,616 exact-term effectiveness records are legislator/chamber aggregates,
not bill-topic labels. The 1,298 presidential speech records are federal
provider-coded units, not state bills or verified human gold; 47 encoding
negatives remain explicit. Neither code zero nor an unflagged compatibility
check becomes a fabricated rejection/independent label. Rights, taxonomy
compatibility and annotation provenance remain unapproved; none was added
to training or evaluation. Full raw bytes and complete records are retained,
not merely links or metadata counts.

## Bounded native-list recovery and all-state catalog

The previously unassigned inventory states now have an actual bounded
official-source recovery record, not another mocked collector pilot.
One shared capture/declarative replay mechanism retains complete publisher
fields, literal source dates, every physical request/error/redirect,
raw entities and persistent limits. Historical use remains unknown;
newly authorized increments are not a claim that earlier usage was zero.
Accepted closed scopes were not recollected.

| State | Effective source observations / identities | Actual recovered scope |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| AR | 1,991 / 1,991 | All four 2023/2024 regular/fiscal/extraordinary publisher sessions: 119 pages, 29 type groups; seven old pages reused |
| LA | 5,300 / 3,996 | All 37 publisher type queries across six regular/extraordinary sessions; 1,304 ACT-subset repetitions preserved |
| NH | 1,996 / 1,996 | Complete returned 2023/2024 queries, 675 and 1,321 records; not an independently verified introduced universe |
| ND | 1,006 / 1,006 | Complete embedded regular/special publisher lists; literal action dates are not introduction dates |
| NV | 1,156 / 1,106 | Three complete query lists plus 50 preserved prefix repetitions; literal starred identities are not silently repaired |
| HI | 262 / 262 | Passed-bill subset only; 39,499 filename entries remain a separate reference inventory, not recovered policy texts |

ID discovery is robots-restricted; NE/VT census endpoints are disallowed;
the actual RI bill-status origin disallows access to `/`. MS stopped on
TLS verification failure, not a proven robots prohibition. WY stopped
conservatively on a reCAPTCHA JavaScript literal, not a proven WAF denial
or solved challenge. These are explicit unrecovered scopes, not zero
legislative activity. AL/SC prior discovery limits are unchanged.

Two tightly coupled data-recovery bugs were fixed rather than hidden.
Missing `robots.txt` 404/410 responses can be handled only by explicit
operator opt-in; conservative defaults remain unchanged. This implements
the optional unavailable-policy behavior in RFC 9309 section 2.3.1.3,
not independent collection authorization or a bypass of actual
Disallow/auth/rate/challenge restrictions. A second fix validates full
finite publisher page/type graphs and exact evidenced URL aliases, rather
than wrongly requiring each individual page to contain the entire query.
AR's original false-negative diagnostics remain preserved separately.

the future shared collection adapter layer. Platform applicability, event-time
affiliations, label adjudication and classifier release remain separate gates.

backup.

## Organization profiles: missing evidence and undefined outlier comparisons

Actual pre-fix execution records **16 failing regressions**, including real CLI
exports that cannot be read back because they contain no headers. The expanded
20-case suite also fails against the actual SHA-pinned saved baseline
`a09d63b`, not a reimplementation of it. Logs:
`profile_missingness_before_v1.log` and
`profile_missingness_saved_baseline_before_v2.log`.

All three profile/outlier CSVs now retain their schemas, including empty,
single-state, below-floor and entirely null-filtered populations; new runs
replace stale outputs. Gap-only organizations are included rather than silently
dropped. Missing gap metadata and unknown document counts remain nullable,
distinct from an observed zero. The CLI identifies each D/R comparison with
fewer than two eligible states as **unavailable**, not evidence of conformity.

Unknown, nonfinite or negative topic counts raise explicitly instead of being
silently summed into a profile. A zero-total group is not a topic-share vector,
even with a zero sample floor. Outlier inputs must be finite, nonnegative
topic-share distributions summing to one; undefined inputs no longer manufacture
distance 1 or NaN results. Nonpositive null-draw configuration also raises.
The existing leave-one-out equal-state peer mean, sample floors, multinomial
null calculation, random seeds, tie handling and rounding are unchanged.

All **92 targeted emphasis/intra-party/state-focus tests**, Ruff and diff
checks pass in `profile_missingness_complete_v2.log`. An actual old/new
differential executes 36 synthetic platform rows, 18 bill rows and six gap rows.
Current-era and pooled-era profiles, three topic-vector populations, and six
raw/null-filtered outlier comparisons agree exactly, including dtypes, column
order and CSV bytes. Both real CLIs produce identical supported stdout after
normalizing only their distinct output paths, and all three CSV exports match
byte-for-byte. Receipt `profile_missingness_supported_differential_v1.json`,
SHA `ab970efb06c3c94ad8a9c3272f19484fdff64ebdb6f699f81a812f55143b64b4`.
Temporary synthetic inputs/outputs are removed. No actual research outputs,
source evidence, labels, model weights, scientific admission or publication
files change.

## Outcome inference availability and nullable eligibility

Actual pre-fix execution records **eleven failures and four passing controls** in
`outcome_inference_missingness_before_v1.log`. Zero or one paired state returns
1.0 as though inference were available; zero permutations also produces 1.0.
Nullable classifications raise an ambiguous-truth-value error, a fresh CLI
output directory is not created, and the artifact auditor crashes on a null
probability. Existing report/chart formatting cannot represent unavailable
inference consistently.

Sign-flip inference now returns JSON **null** for fewer than two paired states,
and nonpositive permutation counts raise an explicit configuration error.
Observed one-state differences, including zero, remain descriptive values;
their unavailable inference is not replaced with a chance result. A shared
formatter serves the chart and figure report and suppresses legacy one-state
1.0 values even when reading an older comparison file. The auditor records a
failed probability check for null instead of raising a TypeError.

Missing classifications do not become ordinary bills. An explicit boolean mask
preserves columns for empty eligible populations, the CLI creates its output
directory, and all four CSVs remain header-readable. Comparison JSON uses strict
serialization. The recorded-stage estimand, all-eligible filing denominators,
numerators, coverage floors, seed and supported permutation calculation are
unchanged.

All **85 targeted outcome/plotting/report/null-auditor tests**, Ruff and diff
checks pass in `outcome_inference_missingness_complete_v1.log`. An actual
old/new differential against saved revision
`d0fb7388c5d038323696268e6a82098e7b4f0e4b` executes a 6,005-row synthetic
bill fixture, 24 vote events, 96 party-count rows and ten supported sign-flip
arrays. All four frames, dtypes, column order and CSV bytes match exactly.

comparison JSON. Receipt: `outcome_inference_supported_differential_v1.json`,
SHA `d25dcefad38183786ac4b30d0a378c5f02762b9cfa89f33c166d92bb4612bd1e`.
The temporary fixture is removed. No actual research outputs, source evidence,

## Bill-tag diagnostics: prediction-only classes and unavailable rates

Actual pre-fix synthetic execution records **six failures and two passing
controls** in `tag_agreement_zero_support_before_v1.log`. The report previously
iterated only over tag-reference classes: predictions assigned exclusively to
another class disappeared from its per-topic false-positive/precision accounting.
The figure report also printed `nan%` for classes with no predictions.

The diagnostic now retains the union of reference and predicted classes within
the same eligible mapped-tag population. Prediction-only classes retain their
predicted counts, **zero measured tag precision** and contaminants; their absent
reference denominators yield unavailable recall/coverage, not invented zeros.
Reference-only classes retain their measured recall and unavailable precision.
Abstentions, Montana source-draft exclusions and unmapped-tag exclusions remain
unchanged. Overall eligible counts and agreement numerators do not increase.
The CLI renders unavailable rates without `nan%`; the figure report ranks only
defined precision and explicitly discloses an entirely unavailable ranking.

All **71 targeted bill-tag/plot/report regressions**, targeted Ruff and diff checks
pass in `tag_agreement_zero_support_complete_v1.log`. A separate actual
saved-baseline differential uses **38 synthetic records**, four helpers, all
three CLI CSV exports and both actual report commands. Values, dtypes, columns,
CSV bytes and supported stdout remain identical; fixed prediction/loader and
fixture taxonomy stubs are explicit, with **zero model forward passes**.
Receipt `tag_agreement_supported_differential_v1.json`, SHA
`8189fd58ad8d4ea34339f6bbc472f1f729d31f9d9d7f3f3f1dddde230807d355`,
records the comparison and cleaned private fixture. This does not regenerate
actual research outputs or establish source-tag independence, model accuracy,
scientific certification or publication approval.

### Nullable subject evidence

A separate actual pre-fix run records **eight failures and eight passing
controls** in `nullable_subject_tags_before_v1.log`. Direct scalar and all three
tag-report helpers crash on `pd.NA` subjects in object/nullable-string columns.
Numeric missing values can also become a literal `"nan"` tag if that string is
mapped. The existing CLI's normalization already handles the tested nullable
Parquet input; its eight actual CLI variants are passing controls, not newly
repaired CLI failures.

The shared resolver now recognizes missing scalars before truth testing or
string conversion. Missing subjects stay unmapped, never guessed. Eligible
reference counts, abstentions and the existing ambiguous/unmapped/draft
exclusions are unchanged. All **83 targeted bill-tag/plot/report regressions**,

## Intra-party missingness, undefined ratios and empty exports

Direct execution exposes **eleven initial regression failures**, with one passing
numeric control, in `intraparty_missingness_before_v1.log`. Empty vectors lack
named result schemas; no shared states or only one shared state cause sorting
failures before exports finish. Empty runs skip writing outputs, allowing old
results to survive a new unsupported population. When between-party distance is
exactly zero, the ratio is undefined and CLI formatting raises rather than
preserving the observed zero distances.

A separate failure is substantive: undefined cosine distances produce an
undefined observed dispersion gap, but every NaN comparison is false and the
permutation formula still returns a small numeric p-value. The repaired test
requires finite, nonzero organization vectors before reporting a permutation
result and rejects zero permutations as invalid configuration. This does not
change the permutation design or its computation on supported vectors.

All four table-producing helpers now retain their existing column schemas for
empty results. The CLI always writes all eight CSV artifacts, including readable
empty replacements for stale files, and explicitly reports insufficient shared
samples. A shared formatter preserves observed zero distances and displays
undefined ratios as **unavailable** in both the CLI and figure report. The
report also treats a legacy null gap/p-value as unavailable, not a finding
indistinguishable from chance; that additional pre-fix failure is preserved in
`intraparty_missingness_report_gap_before_v1.log`.

The final **68 targeted intra-party/plotting/report tests**, Ruff and diff checks
pass: `intraparty_missingness_complete_v3.log`. Earlier successful v1/v2 logs
remain retained. Regression cases include empty, one-party, one-shared-state,
zero-between-distance and wholly/partially undefined vector populations, plus
replacement of stale output files.

`5d96f883c5dcae62314ce9257ba38002f1aa5c50` checks a **36-row synthetic

## Classifier numerical decision safeguards

Five executed regressions expose a shared decision-path failure: individually
finite logits and an allowed positive finite temperature can overflow during
scaling, yielding NaN scores. Comparisons against confidence and margin gates
then fail to reject them; a mixed or supervised-rejection input can incorrectly
receive a policy topic. These are synthetic numerical counterexamples, not
evidence that any saved pilot prediction encountered overflow.

The shared decision path now centers the original logits before scaling **only
when the normal scaled values are non-finite**. This produces finite normalized
scores and preserves the mixed-chunk and learned-rejection decisions. The
ordinary finite-scaling calculation is unchanged, as are source inputs,
weights, labels, thresholds, calibration files and scientific status.
All **87 ModernBERT safeguard tests** and targeted Ruff checks pass. The actual
five-case pre-fix failures and completed run remain in
`modernbert_finite_scores_before_v1.log` and
`modernbert_finite_scores_complete_v1.log`. This repairs invalid decisions; it
does not improve measured pilot accuracy or certify the classifier.

## Comparison missingness and matched-state accounting

Direct production inspection and five executed pre-fix regressions reproduced
missing-party bill shares becoming zero, explicit null shares becoming zero,
missing-topic counts remaining null despite a known classified sample, pooled
platform sensitivity inventing a missing party's share, and a global union
state count being reported for each party. The failed run is retained as
`comparison_missingness_before_v1.log`; the original production files and
regression inputs are bound by `comparison_missingness_before_v1.sha256`.

The comparison now distinguishes an absent topic within an observed classified
party sample from an unavailable sample or an explicitly unknown observation.
Only the former receives a zero share and, for bills, a zero topic count.
The output retains each observed topic for both major parties, including
bill-only topics and their source-provided names; conflicting names for the
same topic code fail explicitly. An unavailable side leaves the numerical gap
unavailable. The upstream pooled platform summary and comparison CLI no longer
reintroduce missing-party zeros. Equal-state and pooled bill summaries report
the number of observed states for each party, not the union across parties.

The subject-tag replication had a separate unconditional zero-fill and omitted
model-state availability from its matched-state intersection. It now reuses
the comparison rules across the union of all three streams' topics and matches
states with both D/R observations in platforms, model-labelled bills and
tag-labelled bills. Its `holds` verdict is nullable: an unavailable source
share produces an unavailable verdict, not a failed replication. CSV exports
retain the missing value; the reporting script separately counts unavailable
rows and uses only available verdicts in its replication denominator. Plotting
preserves missing markers and does not flag unavailable verdicts as
contradictions. Known ties still do not support a nonzero directional claim.

All **82** tests in the three affected emphasis, bill-validation and plotting
suites pass, including actual temporary-file CLI/export checks with classifier
test doubles, an empty matched-state case, nullable report denominators and
masked plotted observations. Targeted Ruff checks also pass. The actual final
run is retained as `comparison_missingness_complete_v1.log`. No production
inference, training, label edits or analytical regeneration was performed.
These are accounting repairs, not improved accuracy measurements or new
substantive findings; the v53 audit and frozen legacy outputs remain unchanged.

## Shared retrieval response lifecycle and evidence limits

After the v43 checkpoint, the shared `fetch` path was repaired to close successful,
HTTP-error, retryable and oversized responses after reading or rejecting them,
before another attempt or return. A redirect without a `Location` is no longer
returned already closed by the default transport; its caller owns final cleanup.
This fixes a resource-lifecycle gap without treating an error response as data or
changing retry decisions and source-admission rules.

The helper's receipts summarize logical retrievals, **not every physical request**.
It still does not retain intermediate redirect/retry bodies or HTTP-error bodies,
and its attempt count is not a cross-attempt HTTP request budget. The code guide
and module documentation now state these limits explicitly. Separate bounded
research collectors must continue retaining per-exchange headers/bodies and
enforcing their aggregate allowances; this repair cannot recover previously lost
response bytes or retroactively make earlier collection compliant.

The bounded recovery below has been followed by full acquisition of the official
2023–2024 archive. `data/processed/california-official-full-originals-v3/`
retains the complete **1,261,848,876-byte** ZIP, SHA-256
`71f098005021971d50c8408bfb1a745d053a633cfa81b3c0d454aa4cdd77b9b6`.
Independent offline replay verifies every one of its **204,862** ZIP entries,
including CRC and uncompressed hashes, and checks local/central metadata for the
5,566 used members. All **5,544 regular-session canonical identities** have
exactly one native table/XML candidate typed `Introduced`/`INT`; no candidate is
missing, ambiguous, identity-conflicting or text-failing within that scope.
Twenty special-session candidates remain separate: nine in session 1 and eleven
in session 2. Intent-only and other previously unselected documents are retained,
not removed to improve source or classification coverage.

The packet contains **5,564 complete raw CAML documents and normalized text
candidates**, totalling **55,860,334 characters**: 9,473,855 header characters,
46,375,351 body characters and 11,128 synthetic joiner characters. Bodies include
4,836 `Bill` and 728 `Resolution` nodes. `introduced_candidates.parquet` records
native version/measure identities, exact raw XML/text hashes and paths;
`canonical_dispositions.parquet` preserves every regular canonical join. All
columns of the six filtered canonical input tables survive separately. Current
canonical titles, abstracts and summaries are not prepended to these inputs.

The **16,692 construction spans** are exactly one header, synthetic `\n\n`
joiner and body per candidate. Character offsets address the normalized Unicode
text; header/body byte offsets and hashes address complete nodes in raw XML.
These are **not typed nested legislative-markup spans**. Text and tails inside
nested XML survive the projection with a synthetic space between each text/tail
fragment before whitespace collapse. Insertion,
deletion, strikeout and other markup semantics are flattened. Deleted words are
not identified or removed. Exact markup remains in `originals/raw_caml/`.
Semantic interpretation remains unfinished. The separate native-element inventory
below now supplies nested source offsets without interpreting them as legal operations.

Only **one new transport request** acquired the archive. The exact original
42,104-byte/65-request ledger prefix remains checksum-bound; aggregate collection
is **66/128**, not a reset allowance. The full HTTP 200 body, raw/parsed headers,
receipt and empty stderr are retained. Transport used ordinary verified HTTPS
without redirect following or certificate bypass. Each successful packet has its
own archive path; hard links share retained bytes but do not require the failed
attempt directories to remain addressable.

Full-recovery v1 failed on an existing-directory creation error. Diagnostic v2
incorrectly accepted only `Bill` bodies, missing 728 `Resolution` candidates
(723 regular). Those attempts remain preserved and are not completed recoveries.
Successful v3 manifest SHA-256:
`bd5fc67a78c88eeeda65a7b132a7031c52e5968f91eebf205487f872d818ebd6`.
Parent semantic replay and strict inventory comparison passed with all **11,166**
file hashes unchanged.

“No residual gaps” means complete accounting of the archive's current typed
`Introduced`/`INT` candidates and their regular-session canonical joins. It does
not establish filing-time legal identity, sponsor party, source semantics,

## Full-campaign preparation gates: Pennsylvania, Ohio and Missouri

Pennsylvania's initial immutable preparation and seed-32/seed-30 archives have
actual parent guarded replays. Native population **4,786** remains distinct from
**4,788 canonical contexts**. All **62 complete PDF/HTML pairs** are reused;
the historical **536 exchanges**, **136 over the proposed 400 preparation
ceiling**, the earlier **24/12 follow-up violation** and **12 lost redirect
bodies / 101,094 bytes** remain explicit. The proposed full **25,000** ceiling
includes every inherited exchange, not a new allowance.

Exact-population testing exposed a real v1 grouping defect: restarting after the
provisional seed-tail 30 produces the remaining population's tail 20 instead of
the full population's tail **18**. The immutable v2 supplement fixes carry and
uncheckpointed-completion recovery. Parent guarded replays verify its **580 files**
and separate **195-file final-18 fixture** unchanged; the positive driver fixture
records **4,724 new candidates / 9,448 mocked PDF/HTML responses**, no seed
refetches and genuine dependency-closed final-18 outputs. These are offline
fixtures, not collected Pennsylvania originals.

Full launch is still withheld. Parent code inspection found further v2 problems:
midstream transport errors can discard already observed bytes/headers; HTTP 429
does not stop immediately; a crash between durable reservation append and
progress save can undercount/reuse a reservation; and exhausted or malformed
formats can leave partial candidates while still sealing a normal success-only
tail. The immutable v3 supplement corrects these paths and its positive
full-population fixture retains the genuine final-18 closure. Actual parent
guarded replays pass for the **658-file supplement / 269-file final-18 archive**,
manifest
`2255619fcc1be2d24dccaaf479f787efdf7d00342a5ddc9b8c81671957967eab`.

## Texas 88R filed-census and bounded original-text recovery

`data/processed/texas-official-parallel-recovery-v3/` retains complete official
House/Senate filed reports for the 88th regular session: **11,807 identities**
(8,285 House; 3,522 Senate). The reports enumerate HB 5,413; HCR 123; HJR 206;
HR 2,543; SB 2,633; SCR 62; SJR 93; SR 734. All are preserved rather than
counting only ordinary bills. Exact joins cover **11,807 of 11,809** canonical
regular contexts with no ambiguity. Literal canonical identifiers **SB 15247**
and **SR 1960** remain unmatched; neither is guessed into another identity or
declared nonexistent.

The deterministic initial cohort retains **16 introduced identities**, each with
one complete official PDF and HTML response: **16 pairs / 32 document bodies**.
`document_pages.parquet` has 71 extraction records, but only **55 physical PDF
pages**; the other **16 HTML blocks are not physical pages**. Exact page/text
hashes and combined-text offsets replay independently. Raw HTML preserves nested
tags and attributes; derived text does not retain insertion/deletion, strikeout
or formatting as typed annotations, nor raw-HTML byte offsets. This is bounded
source recovery, not statewide original-text completeness.

Across all attempts collection used **40/128 physical requests**: 35 HTTP 200,
one HTTP 302, one HTTP 500 and three TLS timeouts without an HTTP response.
All received bodies, including the redirect/error, remain byte-bound; zero-byte
timeout records are explicit. Offline replay checks request numbering, pacing,
budget, response bounds and packet-local body lengths/hashes. It validates the
retained TLS declaration and hashed collector, not a new cryptographic handshake.
The original absolute ledger paths are historical observations; verification
uses retained packet-local copies and needs no external transport directory.

Canonical regular tables retain their original columns. Special sessions 881,
882, 883 and 884 are separately retained (117, 169, 598 and 399 contexts), with
disjoint regular/special IDs. Earlier v1/v2 verifier defects remain preserved:
unequal-length strict zip, a zero-count categorical disposition, and bill-only
header expectations that did not handle resolutions. They added no requests.

Successful manifest SHA-256:
`c0376c019d06084ec1cf74406d3e7095e90bb493f8aff480994f17b87c9eb9ba`.

### Offline repair of false native-HTML negatives

Review of the retained responses found three extraction defects: the legacy text
projection includes head CSS/title text, its bare-number identity gate can miss
zero-padded printed identifiers, and its required closed `tr` blocks mistake Word
paragraph-based bills for unavailable HTML. The pinned running collector and its
original dispositions are preserved, not edited in flight.

The successor `illinois_html.py` preserves complete body characters/entities and
mapped `br` newlines, with exact decoded-source/output offsets and literal tag
attributes. It records lexical tokens, not guessed DOM parents: native XSL has
unmatched child tags, and Word bodies need not contain closed table rows. Head
CSS/titles stay outside the projection; struck, underlined and CSS-hidden words
remain present without certified legal or visibility interpretation. Active,
conditional and non-text bodies fail explicitly. A separate anchored printed
GA/year/typed-number observer preserves `HB0015` rather than seeking incidental
bare `15` elsewhere in the bill. This is not legal-version certification.

The immutable diagnostic covers **every negative in the first 51 completed
32-candidate batches**: **578 identity-unverified** and **103 unavailable-shell**
records, plus HB2/HB3 positive controls. All **683 complete native responses**
yield nonempty bodies and matching literal typed headers: **901,346 body
characters / 375,559 lexical spans**. Independent replay reproduces both the
old negative observations and the new complete body text, including a separate
body reader and every source/output span. All **8,991 file hashes** remain
unchanged with networking and original project/session data reads denied.
Twenty-four focused cases and 68 combined IL/TX/NY/CA regressions pass.

Root: `data/processed/illinois-native-html-negative-replay-v1/`.
Manifest SHA-256:
`6d13422e521cf6d0e440ad328974a05d500541bfbead9e962d5db896658e03ef`.
This replay makes **zero requests**, preserves all original campaign states and
does not represent the still-running full campaign as complete. It uses the same
reversible ISO-8859-1 byte projection as the old collector; actual source encoding
is **not certified**, and these are not admitted classifier inputs, labels,
training/evaluation data or analytical/publication evidence.

An additional full-cohort decoding replay establishes a narrower positive result:
**580 responses are entirely ASCII**, and all **103 Word responses explicitly
declare Windows-1252**. Their only non-ASCII byte is `0xA0` (**2,072
occurrences**), which decodes identically under Windows-1252 and Latin-1.
Every response declares only `text/html` in its HTTP headers. Strict
ASCII/declared-Windows-1252 decoding reproduces **every complete source string,
saved body, lexical span and header** without replacement or a guessed codec.
All 8,991 files remain unchanged on both runs. This resolves decoding equivalence
for this fixed cohort, not actual charset ground truth or future responses.

## Withdrawal-only source inputs: production evidence abstention

The production bill-input path now recognizes the complete reviewed Missouri
2024 Senate withdrawal-only cover and returns
`withdrawal_notice_no_policy_text` before topic inference. These are missing
policy-text inputs, **not non-policy classifications or exclusions of historical
measures from the coverage denominator**. No unaligned title/summary fallback is
allowed. Full introduced-text candidates, hashes, original canonical fields and
native header/code observations remain in record exports.

Recognition is deliberately narrow: the observed session/assembly, explicit
withdrawal markers, Senate header, sponsor, W print code and secretary must occupy
the complete text. Appended policy provisions and ordinary withdrawal-related
legislation do not match. A differing canonical identifier is retained, not
silently repaired from the observed header. Other states are outside this parser's
scope; this is not a claim to detect every non-substantive publisher format.

`data/processed/missouri-withdrawal-input-guard-verification-v1/` independently
re-extracts all five complete one-page PDFs for SB961, SB1121, SB1122, SB1171 and
SB1339, matches every character to the retained texts and exercises the production
input path with the exact canonical contexts. All five abstain without a model
forward pass. The packet preserves the full parent source snapshot, candidate
inputs, guard outputs and exact production-source bindings. Its manifest SHA-256
is `63cc130a8d00d81ce7efffc5bfc18ac53274a2a3a50dabdc922a006b1987bbe0`.
The separate read-only replay passed without changing packet bytes. Focused
regressions also retain notices in coverage and full downloads rather than
inflating the classification rate by removing missing-evidence cases.

These are guard probes, not admitted predictions. No canonical introduced text,
labels, model weights, measured accuracy, historical affiliations, analytical
findings or publication files changed. Zero additional substantive original
policy documents were recovered by this repair.

Actual canonical audit v43 binds this guard packet and its production source,
tests and replay logs alongside the 99 previously completed packets. Its manifest
SHA-256 is `37c0b7423abf2659ccd7eb4a36c79f26d02dee48d3a539e7b1cabf758eede812`.
The audit completed with return code **1**, **300/334** passing checks and the
same **34** missing-artifact failures. A complete report comparison against v42
finds only the passing/total check counts changed; artifact results, computed
results, scientific non-certification and every failure are unchanged.
The frozen documentation precedes this result paragraph and top-line update.
The ignored source/probe/audit packets remain local, not backed up by a Git push.

## Official Washington bill-source recovery

The October 19, 2026 recovery retains the complete **24,779** official Washington
bill-version records returned by the Legislature's introduced-since-2017 operation,
**791** historical roster rows across 2017-18 through 2025-26, service documentation
and all eight successful retrieval receipts. Original XML bytes, request scope and hashes
are frozen in `data/processed/washington-official-legislative-recovery-v1/`.
There are **17,472** zero-substitute/zero-engrossment source rows and **17,464** distinct
original bill keys; duplicated originals are retained rather than silently deduplicated.

`data/processed/washington-official-bill-reconciliation-v1/` preserves all **14,143**
baseline Washington measures and joins **13,479** to a unique original official record
using exact session and bill identifiers. An exact biennium, numeric prime-sponsor ID
and original-chamber join then supplies **8,773 D / 4,706 R session-roster observations**.
No fuzzy sponsor-name join, current-roster backfill or invented membership interval is
used. **12,735** matched records include a legal title, retained alongside the official
short/long descriptions and the original baseline title. These are retrieval-time
version-specific source fields, not certified full bill text or filing-time text.

**658** baseline measures lack a unique original in the bulk response, and **six**
have multiple originals. The missing-original population includes **414 SR, three HR,
128 HB, 109 SB, two SJM, one SJR and one SCR**. A sponsor ID can occur in both chambers
after a midterm chamber change; some former-chamber entries are blank. Joining on ID
alone would erase this distinction. The implementation retains chamber boundaries,
blank/duplicate roster uncertainty and every original source-record position.

The **3,979** source-only original records are not automatically missing analytical bills.
They comprise **1,513 gubernatorial appointments**, **2,266 other pre-2018
introductions**, and **200 other post-2017 source-only measures** requiring reconciliation.
These are mutually exclusive descriptive dispositions, not admission decisions. Complete
source-only records and all baseline nonmatches remain downloadable local packet files.

An independent XML replay in
`data/processed/washington-official-reconciliation-verification-v1/` checks every extracted
record field, all 14,143 decisions, exact baseline-column preservation, every source-only
index and all packet hashes. This is programmatic reproduction, **not independent
semantic review or event-time affiliation certification**. All 14,143 Washington
canonical parties remain unknown; the national 39 D / 21 R totals are unchanged.
No analytical, model-training or publication admission follows from these new observations.

Bounded alternate-operation probes are separately retained in
`data/processed/washington-official-gap-probes-v1/`: six successful responses and one
HTTP 500 failure. A direct resolution lookup returns an original excluded by the bulk
date filter because its introduction date is the publisher placeholder
`0001-01-01T00:00:00`; this is not an actual event date. The sampled missing HB/SB
lookups instead return engrossed versions, not original text. A separate planned
417-resolution recovery was planned from these exact measured gaps; its completed
successor is recorded below.

Frozen manifest identities:

- Official retrieval: `fe98a43d5add0dcc058f9d80f256f36a1dfeddf76a32af45b4fda9d35aa47ecb`.
- Bill reconciliation: `f16b3b1e29e7fb3f02c7ea5c0bda00fcc0a3cd107c4d864aa79f89cf478b04ee`.
- Independent replay: `d4e1c0e182d00cd84f20a4de8c067d3a679925bddfd0036788407f71fa8eea5a`.
- Alternate-operation probes: `fa4556abe9791f60396662bd5ee4828c93a7ff9a0915c85a7d8816e98b5d696c`.

Raw evidence remains Git-ignored local data; pushing code and recorded hashes does not
remotely back up these source bytes. The companion remains unpublished and unintegrated.

The canonical full audit checkpoint **v14** still returns **1**, with **34/324**

### Resolving the date-filtered resolution gap

The completed exact-lookup packet
`data/processed/washington-official-resolution-recovery-v1/` retains **417/417**

successor, `data/processed/washington-official-bill-reconciliation-v2/`, preserves all
earlier source records and all 14,143 baseline references; **413** additional originals
are recovered. All 413 carry the publisher introduction-date placeholder, which is
explicitly counted and never converted to an actual event date.

The combined **25,196** source-version records now yield **13,892** unique original
matches and **9,034 D / 4,858 R session-roster observations**. **245** baseline measures
still lack an original, and six retain duplicate-original ambiguity. Three SR and one
HR remain in the missing population because their direct lookups return amended rather
than original versions. Successful retrieval therefore does not imply an original-text
recovery. Legal-title support stays **12,735**, and the original source-only population
and its dispositions do not change.

Every supplemental source is checked against its planned baseline bill, biennium,
chamber, exact query/receipt and complete source hash. All records retain both the global
ledger index and original file/local-row positions. A separate independent XML replay
again checks all 14,143 decisions and every extracted field. No canonical event-time
party, classification, analytical input or publication status changes.

the service requires **billId**. The corrected seven-request probe packet v2 retains

claiming publisher unavailability or inventing party evidence from names. A follow-up
native-sponsor recovery for the exact **251** remaining uncertain measures was planned

### Native primary sponsorship and a complete source-session ledger

All **251/251** native sponsorship queries succeeded, preserving **1,997** complete
parsed source rows and their original XML. The requested **original bill identifier**
binds each list, even when the detailed legislation API has only an amended version
or duplicate-original metadata. The producer uses explicit **Primary** type, not the
first returned row or `Order=0`. Unknown primaries cannot be rescued by cosponsors;
unknown sponsor types, chamber conflicts, conflicting parties and missing/ambiguous
roster entries remain unresolved.

The initial strict reconciliation left six repeated-primary-ID cases unknown. Reading every
remaining case showed the repeated primary IDs actually agree, with the same chamber
and source party. The successor retains **every repeated row**, per-row roster proofs
and explicit duplicate-ID counts rather than silently deduplicating them or inventing
a disagreement. All six duplicate-original legal-text ambiguities still remain; source
sponsorship does not certify which original text version is intended.

unchanged because its original-record-only evidence contract has not changed.
All canonical `sponsor_party` and event-time `filing_time_party` values remain unknown.
The resolver source is copied and checksum-bound alongside the complete parent and
native-source packets.

The eleven remaining native gaps were individually read: **ten** source titles explicitly

is not evidence that the substantive bill does not exist.

For HB 2379, the retained Open States version-link rows lead to the official original
HTML and **five-page PDF**, successfully recovered through both recorded HTTP and explicit
HTTPS alternatives (**four successful receipts, two distinct bodies**). The first-page
header identifies the bill, 2024 session, sponsors and first reading on **January 15,
2024**. A first-reading date is not automatically a filing date, and the printed sponsor
list is not by itself a dated party-membership interval. The document is not automatically

### Preventing publisher test records from becoming policy evidence

The classifier now excludes the six exact reviewed Washington system-test/training titles,
with whitespace/case normalization and a Washington-only scope. It does not use a generic
testing keyword. The exclusion applies even with attached sample policy summaries or bodies;
Illinois/New Mexico drafting-shell behavior remains unchanged when substantive evidence exists.
Every excluded row retains its source record, exact classification input/hash and explicit
`source_test_record` reason with `classification_status=excluded`; the model is not invoked.

A frozen replay screens all **14,143** Washington baseline titles and flags **exactly the ten
reviewed source IDs**, with no additional baseline rows flagged. All ten source rows and their
exclusion outputs are preserved in
`data/processed/washington-system-test-exclusion-verification-v1/`.
The substantive HB 2379 title is not excluded. The complete baseline hash remains unchanged.
This checks the bounded reviewed population, not the absence of all other possible test bills
or the accuracy of substantive classifications.

Coverage now reports `n_source_test_excluded` separately, removes these records from the
substantive denominator and does not misreport them as model abstentions. Source/prediction
inventories retain them, and the separate count reaches the state-party atlas and capability
report. Existing coverage artifacts are not silently rewritten; no model training, canonical
analysis or publication admission occurs.

Exclusion verification manifest:
`07dae8272fdb643c7aea25a2bf51ca47ddab7951814a84a8e749893babb626c7`.

Canonical audit **v17** records **28** completed research packets and still returns **1**
with the same **34/324** failures. Its manifest is
`ff2a2f641bd81fc7e59f404fc21d57302a3215e8bb5672215c6e8bd83a50d6af`;
the canonical report remains unchanged.

### Original-document recovery and a version-note conflict

The completed original-link campaign follows **389 retained references across 249** of
the exact **251** native-recovery targets. **407 requests** include explicit conditional
HTTPS alternatives, yielding **371 retained responses for 240 measures** and **36 HTTP
404 failures**. Every failure belongs to nine previously reviewed system-test records;
the ten test records together include one without an original-note link. These are
bounded request outcomes, not proof that the publisher has no other documents.

Strict extraction retains **241 HTML / 130 PDF** full texts and **1,809 PDF pages**.
The **9,163,509** extracted characters include parallel formats and are not unique policy
volume. All raw bytes, complete text files, source-version/link rows and unsuccessful
receipts remain in immutable packets. **366** automatic native-heading/session matches
cover **237** measures. Five resolution-format exceptions across three more measures
have explicit assistant source-scope reviews, bringing supported original-source context
to **240 measures** without changing any earlier automated result.

Those exceptions are 2017-2018 SR 8678, 2023-2024 SR 8601 and 2025-2026 HR 4607.
SR 8678 has only publisher-path session support, not a fabricated document date.
The 2023 SR 8601 retains deleted earlier years and underlined replacements; plain text
alone cannot distinguish operative from deleted language. HR 4607 uses its native
year-prefixed resolution heading rather than an ordinary bill-cover format.
These are source-scope reviews, not independent semantic or filing-time certification.

The remaining substantive original-note gap, **2019-2020 SR 8601**, has two retained
references, not four. Both official HTML bodies were successfully recovered. The imported
**Substitute Resolution** note conflicts with the first document's plain **SENATE
RESOLUTION 8601** heading. The other document explicitly prints **ENGROSSED SENATE
RESOLUTION E8601**, agreeing with its imported note. Both adoption clauses refer to the
2019 session, but the added rules-heading year is **2018** in the plain-heading version
and **2019** in the engrossed version. Complete texts, native markup, exact source notes,
header excerpts and the discrepancy are preserved separately. A plain heading does not
by itself prove earliest filing-time text; no source note is overwritten or original
version certified from this contrast.

Across these two retrieval packets, **409 requests / 373 retained response bodies**
now provide native documents for **all 241 non-test measures in this bounded cohort**.
This is not 373 unique bills, all-Washington full-text coverage, or automatic admission
to classification. Original-source scope remains supported for 240 measures, with one
explicit version-identity conflict. This text/version issue is separate from **2023-2024
HB 2379's native-primary sponsorship gap**. The detailed API's **245 missing / six
duplicate original-record decisions** remain unchanged; document recovery does not
silently rewrite those metadata results or historical party affiliation.

All five are under `data/processed/`, with copied producers and explicit source/hash

No canonical baseline, model weights, analytical results or site integration changes.

## All-state document-reference inventory and retrieval

The persistent `state_politics.bills.documents` pipeline now inventories **all 1,090,369**
retained source-context measures, **1,899,909** version records and **2,617,690** links
from the checksum-verified completed October extraction. Every link survives, including
duplicate URLs and later/unclassified version notes. No version records lack a link in
this snapshot. Exact bill/version identity conflicts fail rather than cross-attaching text.

The immutable first pass flags **143,688** whitespace-bearing URLs. Schema-2 successor
v2 preserves each original string while recording a separately encoded request URL,
making syntactically usable references explicit for **37,431 additional measures**.
This is not 37,431 new downloads: v2 has **1,064,790** source-context measures with a
usable request URL, including the ten reviewed publisher tests. It retains **21,252**
measures without a version link and **4,327** with only unsupported FTP references
(**6,522 links**). These are snapshot/reference limitations, not publication absences.
All **752,872 original-note, 439,026 later-note and 1,425,792 unclassified-note links**
remain candidates; original-note hints are not native-version truth.

The reusable collector accepts an explicit manifest-bound link-ID batch, preserves raw
bytes and failure receipts, records HTTPS alternatives separately, skips reviewed source
tests and returns nonzero for unresolved links. An eight-state whitespace-URL smoke
capture retains **seven responses and one Oregon DNS failure**, across **eight receipts /
nine transport attempts**. This is a small retrieval exercise, not coverage certification.

Reading native formats shows why successful requests must not become classifier inputs
automatically. **SD and WY return HTML application shells**, not native bill text; WY's
referenced PDF filename also differs from the baseline chamber identifier and remains
unresolved. **AZ is a proposed strike-everything amendment**, and **UT is an automatic
comparison carrying its own inaccuracy disclaimer**, not an original bill. **NM, OK and
WV** have supported introduced-cover/session contexts in HTML, PDF and DOCX respectively;
DOCX-derived text is explicitly main-document-only.

The Oregon failure was followed through the actual current publisher overview, not
abandoned or repaired by a silent host rewrite. Its native anchors lead to the **2019
SB 9 introduced bill and Senate amendment**, both successfully retained with full
all-page PDF text and separate cover/source reviews. The old DNS failure remains intact.
Complete native responses, full derived text, excerpts, exact notes and review limitations
are frozen; none is independent policy-semantic review or filing-time certification.

### New Mexico native recovery and cross-session quarantine

The bounded New Mexico recovery now retains **all 87 whitespace-bearing references

and attempts embedded extraction across **747 PDF pages**. This is not the complete
New Mexico legislative universe or full independent semantic review.

| SB 17 | Crime and ballistic reporting | Hospital assessment and health-care delivery |

manifest binding. An actual fresh two-link CLI run retained both responses, reported

blanket-blocked; they remain unreviewed and uncertified.

23 pages**, retaining every rendered raster and per-page output. Rendered pages **1, 2
and 23** were visually inspected, not every policy provision. The native heading identifies
a **House Judiciary Committee substitute**, and the publisher warns that amendments
may be proposed but not adopted. Original PDF styling remains necessary: OCR loses
strikeout, underscore and color distinctions. Neither the filename nor the printed
document timestamp establishes filing-time or operative-version status.

and source order for 2,617,690 links**, **all six fields for 1,899,909 version records**,
